diff --git a/contest_fused_moe_gemm/v136_guard_swiglu_padding_rows.py b/contest_fused_moe_gemm/v140_case1_fc1_swiglu_tilelang.py

--- a/contest_fused_moe_gemm/v136_guard_swiglu_padding_rows.py
+++ b/contest_fused_moe_gemm/v140_case1_fc1_swiglu_tilelang.py
@@ -142,6 +142,78 @@ def _swiglu_kernel(
         tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_SAFE_MEMORY_ACCESS: True,
     }
 )
+
+def _fc1_swiglu_case1_kernel(total_padded_tokens):
+    num_experts = 16
+    hidden = 2048
+    intermediate = 8192
+    expert_block_m = 192
+    block_m = 64
+    block_n = 64
+    block_k = 128
+    threads = 256
+
+    @T.prim_func
+    def kernel(
+        stacked_expert_tokens: T.Tensor(
+            (total_padded_tokens, hidden), T.float16
+        ),
+        gate_w: T.Tensor((num_experts, intermediate, hidden), T.float16),
+        up_w: T.Tensor((num_experts, intermediate, hidden), T.float16),
+        group_sizes: T.Tensor((num_experts,), T.int32),
+        group_padded_offsets: T.Tensor((num_experts + 1,), T.int32),
+        activation: T.Tensor(
+            (num_experts, expert_block_m, intermediate), T.float16
+        ),
+    ):
+        with T.Kernel(
+            num_experts,
+            T.ceildiv(expert_block_m, block_m),
+            T.ceildiv(intermediate, block_n),
+            threads=threads,
+        ) as (expert_id, bm, bn):
+            T.use_swizzle(10)
+            token_shared = T.alloc_shared((block_m, block_k), T.float16)
+            gate_weight_shared = T.alloc_shared((block_k, block_n), T.float16)
+            up_weight_shared = T.alloc_shared((block_k, block_n), T.float16)
+            gate_acc = T.alloc_fragment((block_m, block_n), T.float32)
+            up_acc = T.alloc_fragment((block_m, block_n), T.float32)
+            gate_tile = T.alloc_shared((block_m, block_n), T.float16)
+            up_tile = T.alloc_shared((block_m, block_n), T.float16)
+            T.clear(gate_acc)
+            T.clear(up_acc)
+            group_size = group_sizes[expert_id]
+            padded_start = group_padded_offsets[expert_id]
+            for ko in T.Pipelined(T.ceildiv(hidden, block_k), num_stages=1):
+                for i, k in T.Parallel(block_m, block_k):
+                    row = bm * block_m + i
+                    if row < group_size:
+                        token_shared[i, k] = stacked_expert_tokens[
+                            padded_start + row, ko * block_k + k
+                        ]
+                    else:
+                        token_shared[i, k] = T.float16(0)
+                for k, j in T.Parallel(block_k, block_n):
+                    gate_weight_shared[k, j] = gate_w[
+                        expert_id, bn * block_n + j, ko * block_k + k
+                    ]
+                    up_weight_shared[k, j] = up_w[
+                        expert_id, bn * block_n + j, ko * block_k + k
+                    ]
+                T.gemm(token_shared, gate_weight_shared, gate_acc)
+                T.gemm(token_shared, up_weight_shared, up_acc)
+            T.copy(gate_acc, gate_tile)
+            T.copy(up_acc, up_tile)
+            for i, j in T.Parallel(block_m, block_n):
+                row = bm * block_m + i
+                if row < group_size:
+                    gate = gate_tile[i, j]
+                    activation[expert_id, row, bn * block_n + j] = (
+                        gate * T.sigmoid(gate) * up_tile[i, j]
+                    )
+
+    return kernel
+

     intermediate,

import torch
import tilelang
import tilelang.language as T


_KERNEL_CACHE = {}
_WORKSPACE_CACHE_KEY = None
_WORKSPACE_CACHE_VALUE = None
_WEIGHT_CACHE = {}
_EXPERT_BLOCK_M = 192


@tilelang.jit(
    pass_configs={
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_WARP_SPECIALIZED: True,
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_SAFE_MEMORY_ACCESS: True,
    }
)
def _pack_experts_kernel(
    hidden,
    num_experts,
    total_padded_tokens,
    expert_block_m,
):
    block_n = 256
    threads = 256
    packed_shape = (num_experts, expert_block_m, hidden)

    @T.prim_func
    def kernel(
        stacked_expert_tokens: T.Tensor((total_padded_tokens, hidden), T.float16),
        group_sizes: T.Tensor((num_experts,), T.int32),
        group_padded_offsets: T.Tensor((num_experts + 1,), T.int32),
        packed_input: T.Tensor(packed_shape, T.float16),
    ):
        with T.Kernel(
            num_experts,
            T.ceildiv(hidden, block_n),
            threads=threads,
        ) as (expert_id, bn):
            group_size = group_sizes[expert_id]
            padded_start = group_padded_offsets[expert_id]
            for i, j in T.Parallel(expert_block_m, block_n):
                if i < group_size:
                    packed_input[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j] = (
                        stacked_expert_tokens[padded_start + i, bn * block_n + j]
                    )

    return kernel


@tilelang.jit(
    pass_configs={
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_WARP_SPECIALIZED: True,
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_SAFE_MEMORY_ACCESS: True,
    }
)
def _unpack_experts_kernel(
    hidden,
    num_experts,
    total_padded_tokens,
    total_valid_tokens,
    expert_block_m,
    expert_start,
    expert_chunk,
):
    block_n = 256
    threads = 256
    packed_shape = (expert_chunk, expert_block_m, hidden)

    @T.prim_func
    def kernel(
        packed_output: T.Tensor(packed_shape, T.float16),
        routed_expert_weights: T.Tensor((total_valid_tokens,), T.float32),
        group_sizes: T.Tensor((num_experts,), T.int32),
        group_offsets: T.Tensor((num_experts + 1,), T.int32),
        group_padded_offsets: T.Tensor((num_experts + 1,), T.int32),
        out: T.Tensor((total_padded_tokens, hidden), T.float16),
    ):
        with T.Kernel(
            expert_chunk,
            T.ceildiv(hidden, block_n),
            threads=threads,
        ) as (local_expert_id, bn):
            expert_id = expert_start + local_expert_id
            group_size = group_sizes[expert_id]
            raw_start = group_offsets[expert_id]
            padded_start = group_padded_offsets[expert_id]
            for i, j in T.Parallel(expert_block_m, block_n):
                if i < group_size:
                    out[padded_start + i, bn * block_n + j] = (
                        packed_output[local_expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j]
                        * routed_expert_weights[raw_start + i]
                    )

    return kernel


@tilelang.jit(
    pass_configs={
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_WARP_SPECIALIZED: True,
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_SAFE_MEMORY_ACCESS: True,
    }
)
def _swiglu_kernel(
    intermediate,
    num_experts,
    expert_block_m,
):
    block_n = 256
    threads = 256
    shape = (num_experts, expert_block_m, intermediate)

    @T.prim_func
    def kernel(
        gate_output: T.Tensor(shape, T.float16),
        up_output: T.Tensor(shape, T.float16),
        group_offsets: T.Tensor((num_experts + 1,), T.int32),
        activation: T.Tensor(shape, T.float16),
    ):
        with T.Kernel(
            num_experts,
            T.ceildiv(intermediate, block_n),
            threads=threads,
        ) as (expert_id, bn):
            group_size = group_offsets[expert_id + 1] - group_offsets[expert_id]
            for i, j in T.Parallel(expert_block_m, block_n):
                if i < group_size:
                    gate = gate_output[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j]
                    activation[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j] = (
                        gate
                        * T.sigmoid(gate)
                        * up_output[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j]
                    )

    return kernel


@tilelang.jit(
    pass_configs={
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_WARP_SPECIALIZED: True,
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_SAFE_MEMORY_ACCESS: True,
    }
)

def _fc1_swiglu_case1_kernel(total_padded_tokens):
    num_experts = 16
    hidden = 2048
    intermediate = 8192
    expert_block_m = 192
    block_m = 64
    block_n = 64
    block_k = 128
    threads = 256

    @T.prim_func
    def kernel(
        stacked_expert_tokens: T.Tensor(
            (total_padded_tokens, hidden), T.float16
        ),
        gate_w: T.Tensor((num_experts, intermediate, hidden), T.float16),
        up_w: T.Tensor((num_experts, intermediate, hidden), T.float16),
        group_sizes: T.Tensor((num_experts,), T.int32),
        group_padded_offsets: T.Tensor((num_experts + 1,), T.int32),
        activation: T.Tensor(
            (num_experts, expert_block_m, intermediate), T.float16
        ),
    ):
        with T.Kernel(
            num_experts,
            T.ceildiv(expert_block_m, block_m),
            T.ceildiv(intermediate, block_n),
            threads=threads,
        ) as (expert_id, bm, bn):
            T.use_swizzle(10)
            token_shared = T.alloc_shared((block_m, block_k), T.float16)
            gate_weight_shared = T.alloc_shared((block_k, block_n), T.float16)
            up_weight_shared = T.alloc_shared((block_k, block_n), T.float16)
            gate_acc = T.alloc_fragment((block_m, block_n), T.float32)
            up_acc = T.alloc_fragment((block_m, block_n), T.float32)
            gate_tile = T.alloc_shared((block_m, block_n), T.float16)
            up_tile = T.alloc_shared((block_m, block_n), T.float16)
            T.clear(gate_acc)
            T.clear(up_acc)
            group_size = group_sizes[expert_id]
            padded_start = group_padded_offsets[expert_id]
            for ko in T.Pipelined(T.ceildiv(hidden, block_k), num_stages=1):
                for i, k in T.Parallel(block_m, block_k):
                    row = bm * block_m + i
                    if row < group_size:
                        token_shared[i, k] = stacked_expert_tokens[
                            padded_start + row, ko * block_k + k
                        ]
                    else:
                        token_shared[i, k] = T.float16(0)
                for k, j in T.Parallel(block_k, block_n):
                    gate_weight_shared[k, j] = gate_w[
                        expert_id, bn * block_n + j, ko * block_k + k
                    ]
                    up_weight_shared[k, j] = up_w[
                        expert_id, bn * block_n + j, ko * block_k + k
                    ]
                T.gemm(token_shared, gate_weight_shared, gate_acc)
                T.gemm(token_shared, up_weight_shared, up_acc)
            T.copy(gate_acc, gate_tile)
            T.copy(up_acc, up_tile)
            for i, j in T.Parallel(block_m, block_n):
                row = bm * block_m + i
                if row < group_size:
                    gate = gate_tile[i, j]
                    activation[expert_id, row, bn * block_n + j] = (
                        gate * T.sigmoid(gate) * up_tile[i, j]
                    )

    return kernel

def _combine_fc1_weights_kernel(
    hidden,
    intermediate,
    num_experts,
):
    block_i = 64
    block_h = 256
    threads = 256

    @T.prim_func
    def kernel(
        gate_w: T.Tensor((num_experts, intermediate, hidden), T.float16),
        up_w: T.Tensor((num_experts, intermediate, hidden), T.float16),
        combined_w: T.Tensor((num_experts, intermediate * 2, hidden), T.float16),
    ):
        with T.Kernel(
            num_experts,
            T.ceildiv(intermediate * 2, block_i),
            T.ceildiv(hidden, block_h),
            threads=threads,
        ) as (expert_id, bi, bh):
            for i, j in T.Parallel(block_i, block_h):
                row = bi * block_i + i
                col = bh * block_h + j
                if row < intermediate:
                    combined_w[expert_id, row, col] = gate_w[expert_id, row, col]
                else:
                    combined_w[expert_id, row, col] = up_w[
                        expert_id, row - intermediate, col
                    ]

    return kernel


@tilelang.jit(
    pass_configs={
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_WARP_SPECIALIZED: True,
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_SAFE_MEMORY_ACCESS: True,
    }
)
def _combined_swiglu_kernel(
    intermediate,
    num_experts,
    expert_block_m,
):
    block_n = 256
    threads = 256
    combined_shape = (num_experts, expert_block_m, intermediate * 2)
    activation_shape = (num_experts, expert_block_m, intermediate)

    @T.prim_func
    def kernel(
        gate_up_output: T.Tensor(combined_shape, T.float16),
        activation: T.Tensor(activation_shape, T.float16),
    ):
        with T.Kernel(
            num_experts,
            T.ceildiv(intermediate, block_n),
            threads=threads,
        ) as (expert_id, bn):
            for i, j in T.Parallel(expert_block_m, block_n):
                gate = gate_up_output[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j]
                activation[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j] = (
                    gate
                    * T.sigmoid(gate)
                    * gate_up_output[
                        expert_id,
                        i,
                        intermediate + bn * block_n + j,
                    ]
                )

    return kernel


def _get_kernels(
    hidden,
    intermediate,
    num_experts,
    total_padded_tokens,
    total_valid_tokens,
    expert_block_m,
):
    key = (
        int(hidden),
        int(intermediate),
        int(num_experts),
        int(total_padded_tokens),
        int(total_valid_tokens),
        int(expert_block_m),
    )
    kernels = _KERNEL_CACHE.get(key)
    if kernels is None:
        expert_chunk = 32 if num_experts == 32 else 16
        fuse_fc1 = False
        kernels = (
            _pack_experts_kernel(
                hidden,
                num_experts,
                total_padded_tokens,
                expert_block_m,
            ),
            _swiglu_kernel(
                intermediate,
                num_experts,
                expert_block_m,
            ),
            (
                _combine_fc1_weights_kernel(hidden, intermediate, num_experts)
                if fuse_fc1
                else None
            ),
            (
                _combined_swiglu_kernel(
                    intermediate,
                    num_experts,
                    expert_block_m,
                )
                if fuse_fc1
                else None
            ),
            (
                _fc1_swiglu_case1_kernel(total_padded_tokens)
                if num_experts == 16
                and hidden == 2048
                and intermediate == 8192
                else None
            ),
            tuple(
                _unpack_experts_kernel(
                    hidden,
                    num_experts,
                    total_padded_tokens,
                    total_valid_tokens,
                    expert_block_m,
                    expert_start,
                    min(expert_chunk, num_experts - expert_start),
                )
                for expert_start in range(0, num_experts, expert_chunk)
            ),
        )
        _KERNEL_CACHE[key] = kernels
    return kernels


def _get_workspace(
    stacked_expert_tokens,
    intermediate,
    num_experts,
    expert_block_m,
):
    global _WORKSPACE_CACHE_KEY, _WORKSPACE_CACHE_VALUE
    hidden = int(stacked_expert_tokens.shape[1])
    key = (
        int(stacked_expert_tokens.device.index or 0),
        int(num_experts),
        hidden,
        int(intermediate),
        int(expert_block_m),
        str(stacked_expert_tokens.dtype),
    )
    if _WORKSPACE_CACHE_KEY != key:
        _WORKSPACE_CACHE_KEY = None
        _WORKSPACE_CACHE_VALUE = None
        packed_input = torch.empty(
            (num_experts, expert_block_m, hidden),
            device=stacked_expert_tokens.device,
            dtype=stacked_expert_tokens.dtype,
        )
        activation = (
            torch.empty(
                (num_experts, expert_block_m, intermediate),
                device=stacked_expert_tokens.device,
                dtype=stacked_expert_tokens.dtype,
            )
            if False
            else None
        )
        _WORKSPACE_CACHE_KEY = key
        _WORKSPACE_CACHE_VALUE = (packed_input, activation)
    return _WORKSPACE_CACHE_VALUE


def _get_combined_fc1_weight(gate_w, up_w, combine_weights):
    key = (
        int(gate_w.device.index or 0),
        int(gate_w.shape[0]),
        int(gate_w.shape[1]),
        int(gate_w.shape[2]),
        str(gate_w.dtype),
    )
    cached = _WEIGHT_CACHE.get(key)
    if cached is None or cached[0] is not gate_w or cached[1] is not up_w:
        combined_w = torch.empty(
            (gate_w.shape[0], gate_w.shape[1] * 2, gate_w.shape[2]),
            device=gate_w.device,
            dtype=gate_w.dtype,
        )
        combine_weights(gate_w, up_w, combined_w)
        cached = (gate_w, up_w, combined_w)
        _WEIGHT_CACHE[key] = cached
    return cached[2]


def run_kernel(
    stacked_expert_tokens,
    gate_w,
    up_w,
    down_w,
    routed_expert_weights,
    group_sizes,
    group_offsets,
    group_padded_offsets,
    group_idx_for_bx,
    out,
):
    hidden = int(stacked_expert_tokens.shape[1])
    intermediate = int(gate_w.shape[1])
    num_experts = int(gate_w.shape[0])
    total_padded_tokens = int(stacked_expert_tokens.shape[0])
    total_valid_tokens = int(routed_expert_weights.shape[0])

    expert_block_m = _EXPERT_BLOCK_M

    (
        pack,
        swiglu,
        combine_weights,
        combined_swiglu,
        fc1_swiglu_case1,
        unpack_chunks,
    ) = _get_kernels(
        hidden,
        intermediate,
        num_experts,
        total_padded_tokens,
        total_valid_tokens,
        expert_block_m,
    )
    if fc1_swiglu_case1 is not None:
        activation = torch.empty(
            (num_experts, expert_block_m, intermediate),
            device=stacked_expert_tokens.device,
            dtype=stacked_expert_tokens.dtype,
        )
        fc1_swiglu_case1(
            stacked_expert_tokens,
            gate_w,
            up_w,
            group_sizes,
            group_padded_offsets,
            activation,
        )
    else:
        packed_input, activation = _get_workspace(
            stacked_expert_tokens,
            intermediate,
            num_experts,
            expert_block_m,
        )

        pack(
            stacked_expert_tokens,
            group_sizes,
            group_padded_offsets,
            packed_input,
        )
        gate_output = packed_input @ gate_w.transpose(1, 2)
        up_output = packed_input @ up_w.transpose(1, 2)
        swiglu(gate_output, up_output, group_offsets, gate_output)
        activation = gate_output
    expert_chunk = 32 if num_experts == 32 else 16
    for chunk_id, expert_start in enumerate(range(0, num_experts, expert_chunk)):
        expert_end = min(expert_start + expert_chunk, num_experts)
        packed_output = (
            activation[expert_start:expert_end]
            @ down_w[expert_start:expert_end].transpose(1, 2)
        )
        unpack_chunks[chunk_id](
            packed_output,
            routed_expert_weights,
            group_sizes,
            group_offsets,
            group_padded_offsets,
            out,
        )

         tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_SAFE_MEMORY_ACCESS: True,

 )

+def _fc1_swiglu_case1_kernel(total_padded_tokens):
+    num_experts = 16
+    hidden = 2048
+    intermediate = 8192
+    expert_block_m = 192
+    block_m = 64
+    block_n = 64
+    block_k = 128
+    threads = 256
+
+    @T.prim_func
+    def kernel(
+        stacked_expert_tokens: T.Tensor(
+            (total_padded_tokens, hidden), T.float16
+        ),
+        gate_w: T.Tensor((num_experts, intermediate, hidden), T.float16),
+        up_w: T.Tensor((num_experts, intermediate, hidden), T.float16),
+        group_sizes: T.Tensor((num_experts,), T.int32),
+        group_padded_offsets: T.Tensor((num_experts + 1,), T.int32),
+        activation: T.Tensor(
+            (num_experts, expert_block_m, intermediate), T.float16
+        ),
+    ):
+        with T.Kernel(
+            num_experts,
+            T.ceildiv(expert_block_m, block_m),
+            T.ceildiv(intermediate, block_n),
+            threads=threads,
+        ) as (expert_id, bm, bn):
+            T.use_swizzle(10)
+            token_shared = T.alloc_shared((block_m, block_k), T.float16)
+            gate_weight_shared = T.alloc_shared((block_k, block_n), T.float16)
+            up_weight_shared = T.alloc_shared((block_k, block_n), T.float16)
+            gate_acc = T.alloc_fragment((block_m, block_n), T.float32)
+            up_acc = T.alloc_fragment((block_m, block_n), T.float32)
+            gate_tile = T.alloc_shared((block_m, block_n), T.float16)
+            up_tile = T.alloc_shared((block_m, block_n), T.float16)
+            T.clear(gate_acc)
+            T.clear(up_acc)
+            group_size = group_sizes[expert_id]
+            padded_start = group_padded_offsets[expert_id]
+            for ko in T.Pipelined(T.ceildiv(hidden, block_k), num_stages=1):
+                for i, k in T.Parallel(block_m, block_k):
+                    row = bm * block_m + i
+                    if row < group_size:
+                        token_shared[i, k] = stacked_expert_tokens[
+                            padded_start + row, ko * block_k + k
+                        ]
+                    else:
+                        token_shared[i, k] = T.float16(0)
+                for k, j in T.Parallel(block_k, block_n):
+                    gate_weight_shared[k, j] = gate_w[
+                        expert_id, bn * block_n + j, ko * block_k + k
+                    ]
+                    up_weight_shared[k, j] = up_w[
+                        expert_id, bn * block_n + j, ko * block_k + k
+                    ]
+                T.gemm(token_shared, gate_weight_shared, gate_acc)
+                T.gemm(token_shared, up_weight_shared, up_acc)
+            T.copy(gate_acc, gate_tile)
+            T.copy(up_acc, up_tile)
+            for i, j in T.Parallel(block_m, block_n):
+                row = bm * block_m + i
+                if row < group_size:
+                    gate = gate_tile[i, j]
+                    activation[expert_id, row, bn * block_n + j] = (
+                        gate * T.sigmoid(gate) * up_tile[i, j]
+                    )
+
+    return kernel
+

import torch
import tilelang
import tilelang.language as T


_KERNEL_CACHE = {}
_WORKSPACE_CACHE_KEY = None
_WORKSPACE_CACHE_VALUE = None
_WEIGHT_CACHE = {}
_EXPERT_BLOCK_M = 192


@tilelang.jit(
    pass_configs={
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_WARP_SPECIALIZED: True,
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_SAFE_MEMORY_ACCESS: True,
    }
)
def _pack_experts_kernel(
    hidden,
    num_experts,
    total_padded_tokens,
    expert_block_m,
):
    block_n = 256
    threads = 256
    packed_shape = (num_experts, expert_block_m, hidden)

    @T.prim_func
    def kernel(
        stacked_expert_tokens: T.Tensor((total_padded_tokens, hidden), T.float16),
        group_sizes: T.Tensor((num_experts,), T.int32),
        group_padded_offsets: T.Tensor((num_experts + 1,), T.int32),
        packed_input: T.Tensor(packed_shape, T.float16),
    ):
        with T.Kernel(
            num_experts,
            T.ceildiv(hidden, block_n),
            threads=threads,
        ) as (expert_id, bn):
            group_size = group_sizes[expert_id]
            padded_start = group_padded_offsets[expert_id]
            for i, j in T.Parallel(expert_block_m, block_n):
                if i < group_size:
                    packed_input[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j] = (
                        stacked_expert_tokens[padded_start + i, bn * block_n + j]
                    )

    return kernel


@tilelang.jit(
    pass_configs={
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_WARP_SPECIALIZED: True,
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_SAFE_MEMORY_ACCESS: True,
    }
)
def _unpack_experts_kernel(
    hidden,
    num_experts,
    total_padded_tokens,
    total_valid_tokens,
    expert_block_m,
    expert_start,
    expert_chunk,
):
    block_n = 256
    threads = 256
    packed_shape = (expert_chunk, expert_block_m, hidden)

    @T.prim_func
    def kernel(
        packed_output: T.Tensor(packed_shape, T.float16),
        routed_expert_weights: T.Tensor((total_valid_tokens,), T.float32),
        group_sizes: T.Tensor((num_experts,), T.int32),
        group_offsets: T.Tensor((num_experts + 1,), T.int32),
        group_padded_offsets: T.Tensor((num_experts + 1,), T.int32),
        out: T.Tensor((total_padded_tokens, hidden), T.float16),
    ):
        with T.Kernel(
            expert_chunk,
            T.ceildiv(hidden, block_n),
            threads=threads,
        ) as (local_expert_id, bn):
            expert_id = expert_start + local_expert_id
            group_size = group_sizes[expert_id]
            raw_start = group_offsets[expert_id]
            padded_start = group_padded_offsets[expert_id]
            for i, j in T.Parallel(expert_block_m, block_n):
                if i < group_size:
                    out[padded_start + i, bn * block_n + j] = (
                        packed_output[local_expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j]
                        * routed_expert_weights[raw_start + i]
                    )

    return kernel


@tilelang.jit(
    pass_configs={
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_WARP_SPECIALIZED: True,
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_SAFE_MEMORY_ACCESS: True,
    }
)
def _swiglu_kernel(
    intermediate,
    num_experts,
    expert_block_m,
):
    block_n = 256
    threads = 256
    shape = (num_experts, expert_block_m, intermediate)

    @T.prim_func
    def kernel(
        gate_output: T.Tensor(shape, T.float16),
        up_output: T.Tensor(shape, T.float16),
        group_offsets: T.Tensor((num_experts + 1,), T.int32),
        activation: T.Tensor(shape, T.float16),
    ):
        with T.Kernel(
            num_experts,
            T.ceildiv(intermediate, block_n),
            threads=threads,
        ) as (expert_id, bn):
            group_size = group_offsets[expert_id + 1] - group_offsets[expert_id]
            for i, j in T.Parallel(expert_block_m, block_n):
                if i < group_size:
                    gate = gate_output[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j]
                    activation[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j] = (
                        gate
                        * T.sigmoid(gate)
                        * up_output[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j]
                    )

    return kernel


@tilelang.jit(
    pass_configs={
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_WARP_SPECIALIZED: True,
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_SAFE_MEMORY_ACCESS: True,
    }
)

def _fc1_swiglu_case1_kernel(total_padded_tokens):
    num_experts = 16
    hidden = 2048
    intermediate = 8192
    expert_block_m = 192
    block_m = 64
    block_n = 64
    block_k = 128
    threads = 256

    @T.prim_func
    def kernel(
        stacked_expert_tokens: T.Tensor(
            (total_padded_tokens, hidden), T.float16
        ),
        gate_w: T.Tensor((num_experts, intermediate, hidden), T.float16),
        up_w: T.Tensor((num_experts, intermediate, hidden), T.float16),
        group_sizes: T.Tensor((num_experts,), T.int32),
        group_padded_offsets: T.Tensor((num_experts + 1,), T.int32),
        activation: T.Tensor(
            (num_experts, expert_block_m, intermediate), T.float16
        ),
    ):
        with T.Kernel(
            num_experts,
            T.ceildiv(expert_block_m, block_m),
            T.ceildiv(intermediate, block_n),
            threads=threads,
        ) as (expert_id, bm, bn):
            T.use_swizzle(10)
            token_shared = T.alloc_shared((block_m, block_k), T.float16)
            gate_weight_shared = T.alloc_shared((block_k, block_n), T.float16)
            up_weight_shared = T.alloc_shared((block_k, block_n), T.float16)
            gate_acc = T.alloc_fragment((block_m, block_n), T.float32)
            up_acc = T.alloc_fragment((block_m, block_n), T.float32)
            gate_tile = T.alloc_shared((block_m, block_n), T.float16)
            up_tile = T.alloc_shared((block_m, block_n), T.float16)
            T.clear(gate_acc)
            T.clear(up_acc)
            group_size = group_sizes[expert_id]
            padded_start = group_padded_offsets[expert_id]
            for ko in T.Pipelined(T.ceildiv(hidden, block_k), num_stages=1):
                for i, k in T.Parallel(block_m, block_k):
                    row = bm * block_m + i
                    if row < group_size:
                        token_shared[i, k] = stacked_expert_tokens[
                            padded_start + row, ko * block_k + k
                        ]
                    else:
                        token_shared[i, k] = T.float16(0)
                for k, j in T.Parallel(block_k, block_n):
                    gate_weight_shared[k, j] = gate_w[
                        expert_id, bn * block_n + j, ko * block_k + k
                    ]
                    up_weight_shared[k, j] = up_w[
                        expert_id, bn * block_n + j, ko * block_k + k
                    ]
                T.gemm(token_shared, gate_weight_shared, gate_acc)
                T.gemm(token_shared, up_weight_shared, up_acc)
            T.copy(gate_acc, gate_tile)
            T.copy(up_acc, up_tile)
            for i, j in T.Parallel(block_m, block_n):
                row = bm * block_m + i
                if row < group_size:
                    gate = gate_tile[i, j]
                    activation[expert_id, row, bn * block_n + j] = (
                        gate * T.sigmoid(gate) * up_tile[i, j]
                    )

    return kernel

def _combine_fc1_weights_kernel(
    hidden,
    intermediate,
    num_experts,
):
    block_i = 64
    block_h = 256
    threads = 256

    @T.prim_func
    def kernel(
        gate_w: T.Tensor((num_experts, intermediate, hidden), T.float16),
        up_w: T.Tensor((num_experts, intermediate, hidden), T.float16),
        combined_w: T.Tensor((num_experts, intermediate * 2, hidden), T.float16),
    ):
        with T.Kernel(
            num_experts,
            T.ceildiv(intermediate * 2, block_i),
            T.ceildiv(hidden, block_h),
            threads=threads,
        ) as (expert_id, bi, bh):
            for i, j in T.Parallel(block_i, block_h):
                row = bi * block_i + i
                col = bh * block_h + j
                if row < intermediate:
                    combined_w[expert_id, row, col] = gate_w[expert_id, row, col]
                else:
                    combined_w[expert_id, row, col] = up_w[
                        expert_id, row - intermediate, col
                    ]

    return kernel


@tilelang.jit(
    pass_configs={
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_WARP_SPECIALIZED: True,
        tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_SAFE_MEMORY_ACCESS: True,
    }
)
def _combined_swiglu_kernel(
    intermediate,
    num_experts,
    expert_block_m,
):
    block_n = 256
    threads = 256
    combined_shape = (num_experts, expert_block_m, intermediate * 2)
    activation_shape = (num_experts, expert_block_m, intermediate)

    @T.prim_func
    def kernel(
        gate_up_output: T.Tensor(combined_shape, T.float16),
        activation: T.Tensor(activation_shape, T.float16),
    ):
        with T.Kernel(
            num_experts,
            T.ceildiv(intermediate, block_n),
            threads=threads,
        ) as (expert_id, bn):
            for i, j in T.Parallel(expert_block_m, block_n):
                gate = gate_up_output[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j]
                activation[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j] = (
                    gate
                    * T.sigmoid(gate)
                    * gate_up_output[
                        expert_id,
                        i,
                        intermediate + bn * block_n + j,
                    ]
                )

    return kernel


def _get_kernels(
    hidden,
    intermediate,
    num_experts,
    total_padded_tokens,
    total_valid_tokens,
    expert_block_m,
):
    key = (
        int(hidden),
        int(intermediate),
        int(num_experts),
        int(total_padded_tokens),
        int(total_valid_tokens),
        int(expert_block_m),
    )
    kernels = _KERNEL_CACHE.get(key)
    if kernels is None:
        expert_chunk = 32 if num_experts == 32 else 16
        fuse_fc1 = False
        kernels = (
            _pack_experts_kernel(
                hidden,
                num_experts,
                total_padded_tokens,
                expert_block_m,
            ),
            _swiglu_kernel(
                intermediate,
                num_experts,
                expert_block_m,
            ),
            (
                _combine_fc1_weights_kernel(hidden, intermediate, num_experts)
                if fuse_fc1
                else None
            ),
            (
                _combined_swiglu_kernel(
                    intermediate,
                    num_experts,
                    expert_block_m,
                )
                if fuse_fc1
                else None
            ),
            (
                _fc1_swiglu_case1_kernel(total_padded_tokens)
                if num_experts == 16
                and hidden == 2048
                and intermediate == 8192
                else None
            ),
            tuple(
                _unpack_experts_kernel(
                    hidden,
                    num_experts,
                    total_padded_tokens,
                    total_valid_tokens,
                    expert_block_m,
                    expert_start,
                    min(expert_chunk, num_experts - expert_start),
                )
                for expert_start in range(0, num_experts, expert_chunk)
            ),
        )
        _KERNEL_CACHE[key] = kernels
    return kernels


def _get_workspace(
    stacked_expert_tokens,
    intermediate,
    num_experts,
    expert_block_m,
):
    global _WORKSPACE_CACHE_KEY, _WORKSPACE_CACHE_VALUE
    hidden = int(stacked_expert_tokens.shape[1])
    key = (
        int(stacked_expert_tokens.device.index or 0),
        int(num_experts),
        hidden,
        int(intermediate),
        int(expert_block_m),
        str(stacked_expert_tokens.dtype),
    )
    if _WORKSPACE_CACHE_KEY != key:
        _WORKSPACE_CACHE_KEY = None
        _WORKSPACE_CACHE_VALUE = None
        packed_input = torch.empty(
            (num_experts, expert_block_m, hidden),
            device=stacked_expert_tokens.device,
            dtype=stacked_expert_tokens.dtype,
        )
        activation = (
            torch.empty(
                (num_experts, expert_block_m, intermediate),
                device=stacked_expert_tokens.device,
                dtype=stacked_expert_tokens.dtype,
            )
            if False
            else None
        )
        _WORKSPACE_CACHE_KEY = key
        _WORKSPACE_CACHE_VALUE = (packed_input, activation)
    return _WORKSPACE_CACHE_VALUE


def _get_combined_fc1_weight(gate_w, up_w, combine_weights):
    key = (
        int(gate_w.device.index or 0),
        int(gate_w.shape[0]),
        int(gate_w.shape[1]),
        int(gate_w.shape[2]),
        str(gate_w.dtype),
    )
    cached = _WEIGHT_CACHE.get(key)
    if cached is None or cached[0] is not gate_w or cached[1] is not up_w:
        combined_w = torch.empty(
            (gate_w.shape[0], gate_w.shape[1] * 2, gate_w.shape[2]),
            device=gate_w.device,
            dtype=gate_w.dtype,
        )
        combine_weights(gate_w, up_w, combined_w)
        cached = (gate_w, up_w, combined_w)
        _WEIGHT_CACHE[key] = cached
    return cached[2]


def run_kernel(
    stacked_expert_tokens,
    gate_w,
    up_w,
    down_w,
    routed_expert_weights,
    group_sizes,
    group_offsets,
    group_padded_offsets,
    group_idx_for_bx,
    out,
):
    hidden = int(stacked_expert_tokens.shape[1])
    intermediate = int(gate_w.shape[1])
    num_experts = int(gate_w.shape[0])
    total_padded_tokens = int(stacked_expert_tokens.shape[0])
    total_valid_tokens = int(routed_expert_weights.shape[0])

    expert_block_m = _EXPERT_BLOCK_M

    (
        pack,
        swiglu,
        combine_weights,
        combined_swiglu,
        fc1_swiglu_case1,
        unpack_chunks,
    ) = _get_kernels(
        hidden,
        intermediate,
        num_experts,
        total_padded_tokens,
        total_valid_tokens,
        expert_block_m,
    )
    if fc1_swiglu_case1 is not None:
        activation = torch.empty(
            (num_experts, expert_block_m, intermediate),
            device=stacked_expert_tokens.device,
            dtype=stacked_expert_tokens.dtype,
        )
        fc1_swiglu_case1(
            stacked_expert_tokens,
            gate_w,
            up_w,
            group_sizes,
            group_padded_offsets,
            activation,
        )
    else:
        packed_input, activation = _get_workspace(
            stacked_expert_tokens,
            intermediate,
            num_experts,
            expert_block_m,
        )

        pack(
            stacked_expert_tokens,
            group_sizes,
            group_padded_offsets,
            packed_input,
        )
        gate_output = packed_input @ gate_w.transpose(1, 2)
        up_output = packed_input @ up_w.transpose(1, 2)
        swiglu(gate_output, up_output, group_offsets, gate_output)
        activation = gate_output
    expert_chunk = 32 if num_experts == 32 else 16
    for chunk_id, expert_start in enumerate(range(0, num_experts, expert_chunk)):
        expert_end = min(expert_start + expert_chunk, num_experts)
        packed_output = (
            activation[expert_start:expert_end]
            @ down_w[expert_start:expert_end].transpose(1, 2)
        )
        unpack_chunks[chunk_id](
            packed_output,
            routed_expert_weights,
            group_sizes,
            group_offsets,
            group_padded_offsets,
            out,
        )

v140_codex_verify.txt:1754:.\problem_snapshot.json:50:        "text": "```python\nimport torch\n\ndef run_kernel(\n    
stacked_expert_tokens: torch.Tensor,\n    gate_w: torch.Tensor,\n    up_w: torch.Tensor,\n    down_w: torch.Tensor,\n  
  routed_expert_weights: torch.Tensor,\n    group_sizes: torch.Tensor,\n    group_offsets: torch.Tensor,\n    group_pad
ded_offsets: torch.Tensor,\n    group_idx_for_bx: torch.Tensor,\n    out: torch.Tensor,\n) -> None:\n    out.zero_()\n 
   num_experts = int(group_sizes.numel())\n\n    x_f32 = stacked_expert_tokens.to(torch.float32)\n    gate_f32 = gate_w
.to(torch.float32)\n    up_f32 = up_w.to(torch.float32)\n    down_f32 = down_w.to(torch.float32)\n\n    for expert_idx 
in range(num_experts):\n        valid_count = int(group_sizes[expert_idx].item())\n        if valid_count == 0:\n      
      continue\n\n        raw_start = int(group_offsets[expert_idx].item())\n        padded_start = int(group_padded_of
fsets[expert_idx].item())\n\n        x_e = x_f32[padded_start:padded_start + valid_count]\n        gate_logits = torch.
matmul(x_e, gate_f32[expert_idx].transpose(0, 1))\n        up_logits = torch.matmul(x_e, up_f32[expert_idx].transpose(0
, 1))\n        hidden_act = torch.sigmoid(gate_logits) * gate_logits * up_logits\n        y_e = torch.matmul(hidden_act
, down_f32[expert_idx].transpose(0, 1))\n        y_e *= routed_expert_weights[raw_start:raw_start + valid_count].to(tor
ch.float32).unsqueeze(1)\n\n        out[padded_start:padded_start + valid_count].copy_(y_e.to(torch.float16))\n```\n\n这
份参考实现对应的数学语义是：\n\n```text\nfor each expert e:\n    x_e = stacked_expert_tokens[padded_start : padded_start + valid_coun
t]\n\n    gate_logits = x_e @ gate_w[e]^T\n    up_logits   = x_e @ up_w[e]^T\n    hidden      = silu(gate_logits) * up_
logits\n    y_e         = hidden @ down_w[e]^T\n    y_e        *= routed_expert_weights[raw_start : raw_start + valid_c
ount]\n\n    out[padded_start : padded_start + valid_count] = y_e\n```\n\n其中：\n\n- `raw_start = group_offsets[e]`\n- `p
added_start = group_padded_offsets[e]`\n- `valid_count = group_sizes[e]`\n\n实现要点：\n\n**该题目的 GPU 计算算子不能使用 PyTorch，请只用 Ti
leLang 完成相关计算算子。**  \n\n1. `group_offsets` 和 `group_padded_offsets` 需要分别使用，不能混用。\n2. `routed_expert_weights` 的索引基于真实 to
ken 顺序。\n3. `stacked_expert_tokens` 与 `out` 的索引基于 padding 后顺序。\n4. padding 行不参与计算，输出保持为 `0`。\n5. 当前 baseline 主路径会把 `gro
up_idx_for_bx` 用作 block 到 expert group 的映射。\n6. 为了对齐 TileLang kernel 的常见实现，最终结果按 `float16` 写回。\n\n"

p_offsets[expert_idx].item())\n        padded_start = int(group_padded_offsets[expert_idx].item())\n\n        x_e = x_f
32[padded_start:padded_start + valid_count]\n        gate_logits = torch.matmul(x_e, gate_f32[expert_idx].transpose(0, 
1))\n        up_logits = torch.matmul(x_e, up_f32[expert_idx].transpose(0, 1))\n        hidden_act = torch.sigmoid(gate
_logits) * gate_logits * up_logits\n        y_e = torch.matmul(hidden_act, down_f32[expert_idx].transpose(0, 1))\n     
   y_e *= routed_expert_weights[raw_start:raw_start + valid_count].to(torch.float32).unsqueeze(1)\n\n        out[padded
_start:padded_start + valid_count].copy_(y_e.to(torch.float16))\n```\n\n这份参考实现对应的数学语义是：\n\n```text\nfor each expert e:\
n    x_e = stacked_expert_tokens[padded_start : padded_start + valid_count]\n\n    gate_logits = x_e @ gate_w[e]^T\n   
 up_logits   = x_e @ up_w[e]^T\n    hidden      = silu(gate_logits) * up_logits\n    y_e         = hidden @ down_w[e]^T
\n    y_e        *= routed_expert_weights[raw_start : raw_start + valid_count]\n\n    out[padded_start : padded_start +
 valid_count] = y_e\n```\n\n其中：\n\n- `raw_start = group_offsets[e]`\n- `padded_start = group_padded_offsets[e]`\n- `val
id_count = group_sizes[e]`\n\n实现要点：\n\n**该题目的 GPU 计算算子不能使用 PyTorch，请只用 TileLang 完成相关计算算子。**  \n\n1. `group_offsets` 和 `
group_padded_offsets` 需要分别使用，不能混用。\n2. `routed_expert_weights` 的索引基于真实 token 顺序。\n3. `stacked_expert_tokens` 与 `out` 的索
引基于 padding 后顺序。\n4. padding 行不参与计算，输出保持为 `0`。\n5. 当前 baseline 主路径会把 `group_idx_for_bx` 用作 block 到 expert group 的映射。\n6
. 为了对齐 TileLang kernel 的常见实现，最终结果按 `float16` 写回。\n\n"

v140_codex_verify.txt:1959:.\v027_codex_diagnosis.txt:510:候选里只有一个显式线程参数，当前单 kernel 的三处 `T.gemm` 形状分别是 `128x128 @ 64x128

rnel。
v140_codex_verify.txt:1988:.\v027_codex_diagnosis.txt:1143:v024/v025/v026 已排除 `T.copy`、`actual_rows outer if`、`swizzle`
 作为主因。剩余最强信号是 MXMACA/T.gemm lowering 或其自动 copy/sync 在 `thread_binding < 16` 的 16-lane 子组内生成 sync，OJ 的 ThreadSync hoist 
对“部分 block 线程参与同步”路径 native segfault。先诊断线程域是否就是根因。

         tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_SAFE_MEMORY_ACCESS: True,

+def _fc1_swiglu_case1_kernel(total_padded_tokens):
+    num_experts = 16
+    hidden = 2048
+    intermediate = 8192
+    expert_block_m = 192
+    block_m = 64
+    block_n = 64
+    block_k = 128
+    threads = 256
+
+    @T.prim_func
+    def kernel(
+        stacked_expert_tokens: T.Tensor(
+            (total_padded_tokens, hidden), T.float16
+        ),
+        gate_w: T.Tensor((num_experts, intermediate, hidden), T.float16),
+        up_w: T.Tensor((num_experts, intermediate, hidden), T.float16),
+        group_sizes: T.Tensor((num_experts,), T.int32),
+        group_padded_offsets: T.Tensor((num_experts + 1,), T.int32),
+        activation: T.Tensor(
+            (num_experts, expert_block_m, intermediate), T.float16
+        ),
+    ):
+        with T.Kernel(
+            num_experts,
+            T.ceildiv(expert_block_m, block_m),
+            T.ceildiv(intermediate, block_n),
+            threads=threads,
+        ) as (expert_id, bm, bn):
+            T.use_swizzle(10)
+            token_shared = T.alloc_shared((block_m, block_k), T.float16)
+            gate_weight_shared = T.alloc_shared((block_k, block_n), T.float16)
+            up_weight_shared = T.alloc_shared((block_k, block_n), T.float16)
+            gate_acc = T.alloc_fragment((block_m, block_n), T.float32)
+            up_acc = T.alloc_fragment((block_m, block_n), T.float32)
+            gate_tile = T.alloc_shared((block_m, block_n), T.float16)
+            up_tile = T.alloc_shared((block_m, block_n), T.float16)
+            T.clear(gate_acc)
+            T.clear(up_acc)
+            group_size = group_sizes[expert_id]
+            padded_start = group_padded_offsets[expert_id]
+            for ko in T.Pipelined(T.ceildiv(hidden, block_k), num_stages=1):
+                for i, k in T.Parallel(block_m, block_k):
+                    row = bm * block_m + i
+                    if row < group_size:
+                        token_shared[i, k] = stacked_expert_tokens[
+                            padded_start + row, ko * block_k + k
+                        ]
+                    else:
+                        token_shared[i, k] = T.float16(0)
+                for k, j in T.Parallel(block_k, block_n):
+                    gate_weight_shared[k, j] = gate_w[
+                        expert_id, bn * block_n + j, ko * block_k + k
+                    ]
+                    up_weight_shared[k, j] = up_w[
+                        expert_id, bn * block_n + j, ko * block_k + k
+                    ]
+                T.gemm(token_shared, gate_weight_shared, gate_acc)
+                T.gemm(token_shared, up_weight_shared, up_acc)
+            T.copy(gate_acc, gate_tile)
+            T.copy(up_acc, up_tile)
+            for i, j in T.Parallel(block_m, block_n):
+                row = bm * block_m + i
+                if row < group_size:
+                    gate = gate_tile[i, j]
+                    activation[expert_id, row, bn * block_n + j] = (
+                        gate * T.sigmoid(gate) * up_tile[i, j]
+                    )
+
+    return kernel
+

diff --git a/v136_guard_swiglu_padding_rows.py b/v140_case1_fc1_swiglu_tilelang.py

--- a/v136_guard_swiglu_padding_rows.py
+++ b/v140_case1_fc1_swiglu_tilelang.py
@@ -142,6 +142,78 @@ def _swiglu_kernel(
         tilelang.PassConfigKey.TL_DISABLE_SAFE_MEMORY_ACCESS: True,
     }
 )
+
+def _fc1_swiglu_case1_kernel(total_padded_tokens):
+    num_experts = 16
+    hidden = 2048
+    intermediate = 8192
+    expert_block_m = 192
+    block_m = 64
+    block_n = 64
+    block_k = 128
+    threads = 256
+
+    @T.prim_func
+    def kernel(
+        stacked_expert_tokens: T.Tensor(
+            (total_padded_tokens, hidden), T.float16
+        ),
+        gate_w: T.Tensor((num_experts, intermediate, hidden), T.float16),
+        up_w: T.Tensor((num_experts, intermediate, hidden), T.float16),
+        group_sizes: T.Tensor((num_experts,), T.int32),
+        group_padded_offsets: T.Tensor((num_experts + 1,), T.int32),
+        activation: T.Tensor(
+            (num_experts, expert_block_m, intermediate), T.float16
+        ),
+    ):
+        with T.Kernel(
+            num_experts,
+            T.ceildiv(expert_block_m, block_m),
+            T.ceildiv(intermediate, block_n),
+            threads=threads,
+        ) as (expert_id, bm, bn):
+            T.use_swizzle(10)
+            token_shared = T.alloc_shared((block_m, block_k), T.float16)
+            gate_weight_shared = T.alloc_shared((block_k, block_n), T.float16)
+            up_weight_shared = T.alloc_shared((block_k, block_n), T.float16)
+            gate_acc = T.alloc_fragment((block_m, block_n), T.float32)
+            up_acc = T.alloc_fragment((block_m, block_n), T.float32)
+            gate_tile = T.alloc_shared((block_m, block_n), T.float16)
+            up_tile = T.alloc_shared((block_m, block_n), T.float16)
+            T.clear(gate_acc)
+            T.clear(up_acc)
+            group_size = group_sizes[expert_id]
+            padded_start = group_padded_offsets[expert_id]
+            for ko in T.Pipelined(T.ceildiv(hidden, block_k), num_stages=1):
+                for i, k in T.Parallel(block_m, block_k):
+                    row = bm * block_m + i
+                    if row < group_size:
+                        token_shared[i, k] = stacked_expert_tokens[
+                            padded_start + row, ko * block_k + k
+                        ]
+                    else:
+                        token_shared[i, k] = T.float16(0)
+                for k, j in T.Parallel(block_k, block_n):
+                    gate_weight_shared[k, j] = gate_w[
+                        expert_id, bn * block_n + j, ko * block_k + k
+                    ]
+                    up_weight_shared[k, j] = up_w[
+                        expert_id, bn * block_n + j, ko * block_k + k
+                    ]
+                T.gemm(token_shared, gate_weight_shared, gate_acc)
+                T.gemm(token_shared, up_weight_shared, up_acc)
+            T.copy(gate_acc, gate_tile)
+            T.copy(up_acc, up_tile)
+            for i, j in T.Parallel(block_m, block_n):
+                row = bm * block_m + i
+                if row < group_size:
+                    gate = gate_tile[i, j]
+                    activation[expert_id, row, bn * block_n + j] = (
+                        gate * T.sigmoid(gate) * up_tile[i, j]
+                    )
+
+    return kernel
+

     intermediate,

246: By balancing high-level abstractions (like `T.copy`, `T.Pipelined`, `T.gemm`) with the ability to annotate layouts or drop to thread primitives (Level 3) when needed, TileLang can be both user-friendly and highly tunable. ===== /data/tilelang-metax/docs/deeplearning_operators/matmul.md =====
1: # General Matrix-Matrix Multiplication with Tile Library
2: 
3: <div style="text-align: left;">
4:     <em>Author:</em> <a href="https://github.com/LeiWang1999">Lei Wang</a>
5: </div>
6: 
7: :::{warning}
8: :class: myclass1 myclass2
9: :name: a-tip-reference
10: 
11:    This document is still **experimental** and may be incomplete.
12:    Suggestions and improvements are highly encouraged鈥攑lease submit a PR!
13: :::
14: 
15: TileLang is a domain-specific language (DSL) designed for writing high-performance GPU kernels. It provides three main levels of abstraction:
16: 
17: - **Level 1:** A user writes pure compute logic without knowledge of or concern for hardware details (e.g., GPU caches, tiling, etc.). The compiler or runtime performs automatic scheduling and optimization. This level is conceptually similar to the idea behind TVM.
18: 
19: - **Level 2:** A user is aware of GPU architecture concepts鈥攕uch as shared memory, tiling, and thread blocks鈥攂ut does not necessarily want to drop down to the lowest level of explicit thread control. This mode is somewhat comparable to Triton's programming model, where you can write tile-level operations and let the compiler do layout inference, pipelining, etc.
20: 
21: - **Level 3:** A user takes full control of thread-level primitives and can write code that is almost as explicit as a hand-written CUDA/HIP kernel. This is useful for performance experts who need to manage every detail, such as PTX inline assembly, explicit thread behavior, etc.
22: 
23: ```{figure} ../_static/img/overview.png
24: :width: 50%
25: :alt: Overview
26: :align: center
27: 
28: Figure 1: High-level overview of the TileLang compilation flow.
29: ```
30: 
31: In this tutorial, we introduce Level 2 with a matrix multiplication example in TileLang. We will walk through how to allocate shared memory, set up thread blocks, perform parallel copying, pipeline the computation, and invoke the tile-level GEMM intrinsic. We will then show how to compile and run the kernel in Python, comparing results and measuring performance.
32: 
33: ## Why Another GPU DSL?
34: 
35: TileLang emerged from the need for a DSL that:
36: 
37: 1. Balances high-level expressiveness (like TVM or Triton) with enough flexibility to control finer details when needed.
38: 2. Supports efficient code generation and scheduling for diverse hardware backends (NVIDIA GPUs, AMD GPUs, CPU, etc.).
39: 3. Simplifies scheduling and memory pipelines with built-in primitives (such as `T.Pipelined`, `T.Parallel`, `T.gemm`), yet retains options for expert-level tuning.
40: 
41: While Level 1 in TileLang can be very comfortable for general users鈥攕ince it requires no scheduling or hardware-specific knowledge鈥攊t can incur longer auto-tuning times and may not handle some complex kernel fusion patterns (e.g., Flash Attention) as easily. Level 3 gives you full control but demands more effort, similar to writing raw CUDA/HIP kernels. Level 2 thus strikes a balance for users who want to write portable and reasonably concise code while expressing important architectural hints.
42: 
43: ## Matrix Multiplication Example
44: 
45: ```{figure} ../_static/img/MatmulExample.png
46: :alt: Matmul Example
47: :align: center
48: 
49: ```
50: 
51: ### Basic Structure
52: 
53: Below is a simplified code snippet for a 1024 x 1024 x 1024 matrix multiplication. It uses:
54: 
55: - **`T.Kernel(...)`** to initialize the thread block configuration (grid dimensions, block size, etc.).
56: - **`T.alloc_shared(...)`** to allocate GPU shared memory.
57: - **`T.alloc_fragment(...)`** to allocate a register fragment for accumulation.
58: - **`T.Pipelined(...)`** to express software pipelining across the K dimension.
59: - **`T.Parallel(...)`** to parallelize data copy loops.
60: - **`T.gemm(...)`** to perform tile-level GEMM operations (which map to the appropriate backends, such as MMA instructions on NVIDIA GPUs).
61: 
62: ```python
63: import tilelang
64: import tilelang.language as T
65: from tilelang.intrinsics import make_mma_swizzle_layout
66: 
67: def matmul(M, N, K, block_M, block_N, block_K, dtype="float16", accum_dtype="float"):
68:     @T.prim_func
69:     def main(
70:         A: T.Tensor((M, K), dtype),
71:         B: T.Tensor((K, N), dtype),
72:         C: T.Tensor((M, N), dtype),
73:     ):
74:         # Initialize Kernel Context
75:         with T.Kernel(T.ceildiv(N, block_N), T.ceildiv(M, block_M), threads=128) as (bx, by):
76:             A_shared = T.alloc_shared((block_M, block_K), dtype)
77:             B_shared = T.alloc_shared((block_K, block_N), dtype)
78:             C_local  = T.alloc_fragment((block_M, block_N), accum_dtype)
79: 
80:             # Optional layout hints (commented out by default)
81:             # T.annotate_layout({
82:             #     A_shared: make_mma_swizzle_layout(A_shared),
83:             #     B_shared: make_mma_swizzle_layout(B_shared),
84:             # })
85: 
86:             # Optional: Enabling swizzle-based rasterization
87:             # T.use_swizzle(panel_size=10, enable=True)
88: 
89:             # Clear local accumulation
90:             T.clear(C_local)
91: 
92:             for ko in T.Pipelined(T.ceildiv(K, block_K), num_stages=3):
93:                 # Copy tile of A from global to shared memory
94:                 T.copy(A[by * block_M, ko * block_K], A_shared)
95: 
96:                 # Parallel copy tile of B from global to shared memory
97:                 for k, j in T.Parallel(block_K, block_N):
98:                     B_shared[k, j] = B[ko * block_K + k, bx * block_N + j]
99: 
100:                 # Perform a tile-level GEMM
101:                 T.gemm(A_shared, B_shared, C_local)
102: 
103:             # Copy result from local (register fragment) to global memory
104:             T.copy(C_local, C[by * block_M, bx * block_N])
105: 
106:     return main
107: 
108: # 1. Create the TileLang function
109: func = matmul(1024, 1024, 1024, 128, 128, 32)
110: 
111: # 2. JIT-compile the kernel for NVIDIA GPU
112: jit_kernel = tilelang.compile(func, out_idx=[2], target="cuda")
113: 
114: import torch
115: 
116: # 3. Prepare input tensors in PyTorch
117: a = torch.randn(1024, 1024, device="cuda", dtype=torch.float16)
118: b = torch.randn(1024, 1024, device="cuda", dtype=torch.float16)
119: 
120: # 4. Invoke the JIT-compiled kernel

 succeeded in 1066ms:

FAMILY_RANKING

1. A: best v140 target. Implement case1-only memory-safe FC1+SwiGLU TileLang fusion that reads `gate_w` and `up_w` separately, never builds retained combined gate/up weights, and writes only `activation`.
2. B: promising but riskier. A full padding-row-aware FC1/FC2 routed GEMM would need broader custom GEMM replacement and could regress versus the current packed dense BMM path.
3. C: useful later. Fusing pack/unpack with GEMM is valid with documented `T.gemm`, but touching FC2/unpack now overlaps the saturated FC2 chunk family and has larger correctness surface.

DUPLICATE_AUDIT

This is not simple FC2 chunk enumeration, not elementwise block-width probing, and not a cache/view cosmetic tweak. It also does not repeat v132/v128 combined-weight BMM: no `combined_w`, no retained `_WEIGHT_CACHE`, no `[gate; up]` materialization.

SELECTED_ARCHITECTURE

Implement a bounded case1-only Family A prototype:

`num_experts == 16`, `hidden == 2048`, `intermediate == 8192`.

Replace this v136 sequence only for case1:

```python
pack(...)
gate_output = packed_input @ gate_w.transpose(1, 2)
up_output = packed_input @ up_w.transpose(1, 2)
swiglu(gate_output, up_output, group_offsets, gate_output)
activation = gate_output
```

with one TileLang kernel:

```python
activation = torch.empty((16, 192, 8192), device=stacked_expert_tokens.device, dtype=stacked_expert_tokens.dtype)
fc1_swiglu_case1(stacked_expert_tokens, gate_w, up_w, group_sizes, group_padded_offsets, activation)
```

Then keep v136 FC2 chunk16 and unpack unchanged.

NUMBERED_MODIFICATION_POINTS

1. Add `_fc1_swiglu_case1_kernel(total_padded_tokens)` using constants `hidden=2048`, `intermediate=8192`, `num_experts=16`, `expert_block_m=192`, `block_m=64`, `block_n=64`, `block_k=128`, `threads=256`, `num_stages=1`.
2. Kernel grid: `T.Kernel(16, T.ceildiv(192, 64), T.ceildiv(8192, 64), threads=256) as (expert_id, bm, bn)`.
3. Allocate `A_shared(64,128)`, `B_shared(128,64)`, `gate_acc(64,64)`, `up_acc(64,64)`, `gate_tile(64,64)`, `up_tile(64,64)`.
4. Compute gate GEMM with guarded A loads: `row = bm*64+i`; load `stacked_expert_tokens[padded_start+row,k]` only if `row < group_sizes[expert_id]`, else zero.
5. `T.copy(gate_acc, gate_tile)`, clear accumulator, compute up GEMM using `up_w`, then `T.copy(up_acc, up_tile)`.
6. Store `activation[expert_id,row,col] = gate * T.sigmoid(gate) * up` only if `row < group_size`.
7. Route case1 before `_get_workspace`; do not allocate or retain `packed_input` for case1.
8. Leave case2 and case3 byte-equivalent to v136 except kernel tuple plumbing.

DATAFLOW_PROOF

For each case1 expert `e`, each valid row `i < group_sizes[e]`, and each expert column `j`, the new kernel computes:

`gate = stacked_expert_tokens[group_padded_offsets[e] + i, :] @ gate_w[e, j, :]`

`up = stacked_expert_tokens[group_padded_offsets[e] + i, :] @ up_w[e, j, :]`

`activation[e, i, j] = gate * sigmoid(gate) * up`

This is the same activation consumed by v136 FC2. Case2 and case3 retain the accepted v136 path.

MEMORY_LIFETIME_PROOF

No combined FC1 weight is allocated or cached. Case1 skips retained `packed_input`; it uses one local `activation` tensor only for the current call. `_WORKSPACE_CACHE_KEY/_VALUE` remains single-entry and is not expanded. Cross-case retained tensor memory does not increase.

EXPECTED_GAIN

Case1 should benefit most: removes pack launch, removes two global FC1 output tensors, removes guarded SwiGLU launch, and avoids computing case1 invalid rows in FC1 activation. Expected range: small to moderate gain if TileLang GEMM lowering is competitive; likely `0.03-0.12 ms` on case1. Cases2/3 expected unchanged.

RULE_COMPLIANCE

Uses documented TileLang constructs from `v140_remote_gemm_evidence.txt`: `T.Kernel`, `T.alloc_shared`, `T.alloc_fragment`, `T.Pipelined`, `T.Parallel`, `T.gemm`, `T.copy`, `T.use_swizzle(10)`. Does not inspect prohibited repos.

CORRECTNESS_RISKS

Main risk is fp16 rounding parity. Mitigation: store `gate_acc` and `up_acc` to fp16 shared tiles before SwiGLU, matching v136鈥檚 fp16 BMM outputs before activation. Official C500 max error gate should remain within previous case1 tolerance.

COMPILE_RISKS

Two sequential `T.gemm` calls in one kernel plus shared fp16 staging may stress layout inference. Keep `block_n=64`, `num_stages=1`, and shared memory around 48 KiB to stay below the 64 KiB C500 limit seen in v046.

PERFORMANCE_RISKS

PyTorch/MACA BMM may still beat hand TileLang GEMM. This is why the change is case1-only: failures or regressions do not disturb the strong E32/E64 baseline.

IMPLEMENTATION_INSTRUCTIONS

Copy `v136_guard_swiglu_padding_rows.py` to `v140_case1_fc1_swiglu_tilelang.py`. Add the new kernel factory and include it in `_get_kernels`. In `run_kernel`, branch first:

```python
if num_experts == 16 and hidden == 2048 and intermediate == 8192:
    activation = torch.empty((num_experts, expert_block_m, intermediate), device=stacked_expert_tokens.device, dtype=stacked_expert_tokens.dtype)
    fc1_swiglu_case1(stacked_expert_tokens, gate_w, up_w, group_sizes, group_padded_offsets, activation)
else:
    packed_input, activation = _get_workspace(...)
    pack(...)
    gate_output = packed_input @ gate_w.transpose(1, 2)
    up_output = packed_input @ up_w.transpose(1, 2)
    swiglu(gate_output, up_output, group_offsets, gate_output)
    activation = gate_output
```

Then reuse v136 FC2/unpack loop unchanged.

FINAL_GATE PASS_TO_IMPLEMENT

codex
PIPELINE_BLOCKER_FIX

Structurally removed. Current v140 uses distinct `gate_weight_shared` and `up_weight_shared`, loads both in one `T.Parallel(block_k, block_n)` loop, then calls `T.gemm(token_shared, gate_weight_shared, gate_acc)` and `T.gemm(token_shared, up_weight_shared, up_acc)`. No remaining single `weight_shared` in the updated file or parent diff.

SHARED_MEMORY_BUDGET

Plausible. Shared fp16 footprint is:
`64x128` token = 16 KiB, two `128x64` weights = 32 KiB, two `64x64` output tiles = 16 KiB, total about 64 KiB. That is tight but structurally consistent with the stated C500 target.

GATE_UP_DATAFLOW

Unchanged. For case1 only, it computes gate and up FC1 from `stacked_expert_tokens[padded_start + row, :]` against `gate_w[e, n, :]` and `up_w[e, n, :]`, then stores `gate * sigmoid(gate) * up` into `activation[e, row, n]`. FC2/unpack still consumes `activation` as before.

SYNCHRONIZATION

No missing explicit synchronization found under documented `T.Pipelined`/`T.gemm` usage. With `num_stages=1`, the loop loads token/gate/up shared tiles before the two GEMMs in the same K iteration. Separate weight buffers avoid the prior planner write-overlap hazard.

MATH_AND_INDEXING

Looks correct for case1 dimensions: hidden 2048, intermediate 8192, K tile 128, N tile 64 all divide exactly. Token rows are guarded by `row < group_size`; invalid rows are zeroed for GEMM input and not stored to activation. Weight indexing matches the parent BMM transpose convention.

E32_E64_PRESERVED

Preserved. The specialized kernel is only selected for `num_experts == 16 and hidden == 2048 and intermediate == 8192`. E32/E64 continue down the v136 pack/BMM/SwiGLU path and existing FC2 chunk behavior.

NO_UNLISTED_DIFF

Confirmed against `v136_guard_swiglu_padding_rows.py`: diff is limited to adding the case1 FC1+SwiGLU kernel, adding it to the kernel tuple, and branching in `run_kernel`. No tracked workspace diff outside the reviewed files was reported by scoped `git diff`.

RISKS

The local `v140_c500_correctness.log` is stale and still records the old `weight_shared` pipeline-planning failure for case1. This review verifies the updated source/diff structurally, not a fresh C500 compile. Shared memory is also right at about 64 KiB, so C500 acceptance still needs actual compile confirmation.

BLOCKERS

No structural blocker found in the updated file.
